## Measurements Mat Files Format
| Unnamed: 0 | Unnamed: 1 | Unnamed: 2 | Unnamed: 3 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| Information | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | The Matlab .mat format stores post processing measurements. This post processing includes the normalization of the measurements. The .mat file format includes a single cell with the following variable information. | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| .Mat File | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | Variable Name | AllthePdps | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | Location | Title | Information contained in the cell location |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,1} | NaN | The Acqusition number |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,2} | NaN | The Record Number |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,3} | NaN | The Acquisition date and time |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,4} | NaN | The record time |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,5} | NaN | The Absolute Time and Date |
| NaN | AllthePdps{:,6} | NaN | The Impulse Response (I,Q) data |
| NaN | AllthePdps{1,7} | Name | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{1,8} | NaN | The name of the measurement data file |
| NaN | AllthePdps{2,7} | MeasNotes | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{2,8} | NaN | Basic information regarding the measurement data |
| NaN | AllthePdps{3,7} | Pncode File | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{3,8} | NaN | The file location for the Pncode file |
| NaN | AllthePdps{4,7} | Data/Time | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{4,8} | NaN | Data and time of the measurement |
| NaN | AllthePdps{5,7} | Carrier Freq | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{5,8} | NaN | Carrier Frequency in Hz |
| NaN | AllthePdps{6,7} | Base PN code length | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{6,8} | NaN | Base PN code length |
| NaN | AllthePdps{7,7} | PN Oversample | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{7,8} | NaN | PN Oversample |
| NaN | AllthePdps{8,7} | Codeword Length | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{8,8} | NaN | Codeword length |
| NaN | AllthePdps{9,7} | Acquire Every N Codewords | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{9,8} | NaN | There are N records in an acquisition or array. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{10,7} | Acqusitions per file | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{10,8} | NaN | It is the number of Array of N Records in a File.  Files of IQ data are concatenated over a run to generate the complete Impulse Response file. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{11,7} | Number of Arrays | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{11,8} | NaN | Number of Arrays provides the same information as the Acqusitions per file |
| NaN | AllthePdps{12,7} | Number of Records | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{12,8} | NaN | One IQ “codeword” of data. The length of the record is the length of the word\*oversampling. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{13,7} | Word Length | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{13,8} | NaN | The word length has the same information as in the Codeword length |
| NaN | AllthePdps{14,7} | WF Increment | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{14,8} | NaN | The time between data points, general 12.5 ns |
| NaN | AllthePdps{15,7} | Acqusition Delay (sec) | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{15,8} | NaN | The program has a delay between listening for and acquiring each record – it is N codewords long (usually 200).  The Acquisition Delay is an additional delay between every array of N records.  The delay is largely to make sure the computer is keeping up with the data streaming. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{16,7} | Max VST Input Level (dBm) | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{16,8} | NaN | This is the max power level allowed by the receiver without compression.  This allows for variable setting of the available dynamic range to optimize sensitivity. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{17,7} | Antenna Gain Notes | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{17,8} | NaN | This contains the information regarding the antenna gains. If the frequency is 2 Ghz, the sentence will be Tx Tecom stationary 2.9 dBi, Rx moving -3.84 dBi. If the frequency is 5 Ghz, the sentence will be Tx Tecom stationary 3.6 dBi, Rx moving -3.26 dBi. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{18,7} | The Ref File used was: | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{18,8} | NaN | The name of the reference file. |
| NaN | AllthePdps{19,7} | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | AllthePdps{19,8} | NaN | NaN |
